00036: def ring(parent, key, center, radius, thickness, axis='x', col=iron):
00037:     points = []
00038:     for i in range(25):
00039:         a = math.tau*i/24
00040:         c, s = radius*math.cos(a), radius*math.sin(a)
00041:         if axis == 'x':
00042:             p = (center[0], center[1]+c, center[2]+s)
00043:         elif axis == 'y':
00044:             p = (center[0]+c, center[1], center[2]+s)
00045:         else:
00046:             p = (center[0]+c, center[1]+s, center[2])
00047:         points.append(p)
00048:     return parent.include(key, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=thickness, color=col))

00025: def block(parent, key, size, pos, col):

00028: def bar(parent, key, radius, start, end, col=iron):
00029:     return parent.include(key, rod(radius, start=start, end=end, color=col))

00031: def ring(parent, key, center, radius, thickness, axis='x', col=iron):
00032:     # Modest path resolution keeps construction economical.
00033:     points = []
00034:     for i in range(25):
00035:         a = math.tau*i/24
00036:         c, s = radius*math.cos(a), radius*math.sin(a)
00037:         if axis == 'x':
00038:             p = (center[0], center[1]+c, center[2]+s)
00039:         elif axis == 'y':
00040:             p = (center[0]+c, center[1], center[2]+s)
00041:         else:
00042:             p = (center[0]+c, center[1]+s, center[2])
00043:         points.append(p)
00044:     return parent.include(key, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=thickness, color=col))

00007: # Estimated reference anchors: anvil (.43,.39), large wheel (.67,.25),
00008: # small wheel (.35,.29), drawer (.60,.71), floor arrow (.13,.71).
00009: # Review: the bench was too distant and its diagonal too shallow.
00010: # Move the camera closer and farther along the bench; retain the 43 mm lens.
00011: # Review: the foreground machinery and drawer were too small and uncropped.

00014: # Reduce fill/daylight powers and add restrained surface variation.

00030: def block(parent, key, size, pos, col):
00031:     return parent.include(key, box(size, center=pos, color=col))
00032: 
00033: def bar(parent, key, radius, start, end, col=iron):
00034:     return parent.include(key, rod(radius, start=start, end=end, color=col))
00035: 
00036: def ring(parent, key, center, radius, thickness, axis='x', col=iron):
00037:     points = []
00038:     for i in range(25):
00039:         a = math.tau*i/24
00040:         c, s = radius*math.cos(a), radius*math.sin(a)
00041:         if axis == 'x':
00042:             p = (center[0], center[1]+c, center[2]+s)
00043:         elif axis == 'y':
00044:             p = (center[0]+c, center[1], center[2]+s)
00045:         else:
00046:             p = (center[0]+c, center[1]+s, center[2])
00047:         points.append(p)
00048:     return parent.include(key, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=thickness, color=col))

00029: def B(a,n,s,p,c):
00030:     return a.include(n,box(size=s,center=p,color=c))
00031: 
00032: def R(a,n,r,p,q,c):
00033:     if sum((p[k]-q[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-10:
00034:         return a.include(n,rod(radius=r,start=p,end=q,color=c))
00035: 
00036: def cable(a,n,pts,r,c):
00037:     clean=[]
00038:     for p in pts:
00039:         if not clean or sum((p[k]-clean[-1][k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-10:
00040:             clean.append(p)
00041:     for j in range(len(clean)-1):
00042:         R(a,n+'_'+str(j),r,clean[j],clean[j+1],c)
00043: 
00044: def finish(n,a):
00045:     return scene.include(n,a)

00028: def asset(name):
00029:     return Asset(name)
00030: 
00031: def cube(a, name, size, center, color):
00032:     return a.include(name, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00033: 
00034: def tube(a, name, points, radius, color):
00035:     clean = [points[0]]
00036:     for p in points[1:]:
00037:         if sum((p[k]-clean[-1][k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-10:
00038:             clean.append(p)
00039:     if len(clean) > 1:
00040:         return a.include(name, sweep_tube(points=clean, radius=radius, color=color))
00041: 
00042: def cyl(a, name, radius, start, end, color):
00043:     return a.include(name, rod(radius=radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))
00044: 
00045: def sphere(a, name, radius, center, color):
00046:     return a.include(name, ball(radius=radius, center=center, color=color))
00047: 
00048: def rounded(a, name, size, center, color, radius=.01):
00049:     # Every radius is capped against the smallest actual dimension.
00050:     r = min(radius, min(size)*.45)
00051:     return a.include(name, fillet_box(size=size, radius=r, center=center, color=color))

00032: def R(a, n, r, p, q, c):
00033:     if sum((p[k]-q[k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-10:
00034:         return a.include(n, rod(radius=r, start=p, end=q, color=c))

00036: def cable(a, n, pts, r, c):
00037:     clean = []
00038:     for p in pts:
00039:         if not clean or sum((p[k]-clean[-1][k])**2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-10:
00040:             clean.append(p)
00041:     for j in range(len(clean)-1):
00042:         R(a, n+'_'+str(j), r, clean[j], clean[j+1], c)
